<figcaption class="orbit-caption text-center">
<h1>Become an Ordained Minister</h1>
<h2>Get Ordained Online, Officiate A Wedding</h2>
</figcaption>
</figure>
<p>
        The <strong>Universal Life Church</strong> (ULC) is a multi-denominational religious organization with millions
        of members all over the world. Over the decades, the ULC has garnered global recognition for its promotion of
        universal togetherness and religious expression around the world. By becoming an ordained minister of the
        Universal Life Church, you'll be granted all the rights and privileges afforded to clergy members of other
        faiths – including the ability to perform legal wedding ceremonies. Ordination also opens the door for you to
        start a personal ministry, preside over other religious and spiritual ceremonies, and much more.
    </p>
<p>
        Membership is open to anyone who feels called to join; the ULC welcomes individuals of all backgrounds and
        belief systems into the fold. Plus, unlike traditional religious organizations, we don't require years of
        training or expensive courses to become a member of the clergy. Becoming ordained is free, and can be done
        entirely online.
    </p>
<h2>How to Become a Minister</h2>
<p>
        The Universal Life Church believes that all people are naturally endowed with the right to control their own
        spiritual life, and thus that all those who feel so-called should have access to ordination.
    </p>
<p>
        Ordination at the Universal Life Church is completely legal, is cost-free, and isn't complicated. An ancient
        Chinese proverb instructs us that the journey of a thousand miles begins with a single step. Within you lies
        the capacity to make the universe a better place, and your ordination may well unlock that potential. You can
        take your first step today by completing and submitting your online ordination application now.
    </p>
<button class="button large goto-form">Become an Ordained Minister</button>
<h2>Minister Training</h2>
<p>
        We're proud to offer support to a fast-growing community of ministers in all of their endeavors. To that end,
        we've created a collection of minister training materials here on our site. These helpful resources go in-depth
        to provide advice and instruction on a whole host of ministerial duties. We also operate a blog to keep our
        ministers up-to-date on relevant current events and offer interesting topics of discussion.
    </p>
<p>
        While ULC ordinations can serve a variety of purposes, many ministers choose to use their ordination perform
        wedding ceremonies. Sound intimidating? Not to fear, solemnizing a marriage is not as complicated as it might
        sound. We've created a simple guide, found below, that explains the process.
    </p>
<h2>Steps to Performing a Wedding</h2>
<ol class="steps">
<li>
<h3>Contact Marriage License Authority</h3>
<p>
                Once you are <a class="goto-form">ordained</a>, but before performing the ceremony, you may need to obtain documentation proving
                you are a licensed marriage officiant. These policies vary by state, and sometimes even from county to
                county. For more information about the rules in your area, visit our
                <a href="https://www.ulc.org/wedding-laws">State Marriage Laws</a> page.
            </p>
<p>
                Most places will request some basic documentation such as an ordination credential, a letter of good
                standing, or signed statements of ministry. You can find all of these items, along with a host of other
                minister supplies, in the <a href="https://www.ulc.org/minister-store">Minister Store</a> section of this site.
            </p>
<div class="grid-x grid-padding-x">
<div class="cell small-6 medium-4 large-4 text-center pointer link">
<img alt="Minister Training" class="thumbnail" src="/assets/ulc/images/resources/minister-training-87d05c94d8.jpg"/>
<a href="/training/ordination-training"><h3 class="h6">Minister Training</h3></a>
</div>
<div class="cell small-6 medium-4 large-4 text-center pointer link">
<img alt="Wedding Laws" class="thumbnail" src="/assets/ulc/images/resources/wedding-laws-f208aedd26.png"/>
<a href="https://www.ulc.org/wedding-laws"><h3 class="h6">Wedding Laws</h3></a>
</div>
<div class="cell small-6 small-offset-3 medium-4 medium-offset-0 large-4 text-center pointer link">
<img alt="Church Supplies" class="thumbnail" src="/assets/ulc/images/resources/church-supplies-4dd9f650c9.jpg"/>
<a href="https://www.ulc.org/minister-store"><h3 class="h6">Church Supplies</h3></a>
</div>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<h3>Perform the Wedding Ceremony</h3>
<p>
                Ministers of the Universal Life Church are frequently called upon to perform a wedding — from formal
                and traditional ceremonies to laid-back weddings with fun themes, we've seen it all. We always
                encourage creativity and applaud unique ideas as you custom-tailor the ceremony to the particular needs
                and wishes of the couple for their big day. However, as the minister, you should be familiar with the
                basic requirements that <strong>all</strong> ceremonies must meet.
            </p>
<p>
                For a marriage to be legally binding, the ceremony must include two key elements:
            </p>
<ul>
<li>
                    The Declaration of Intent — known as the "Do you take... I do" exchange.
                </li>
<li>
                    The Pronouncement, where you will pronounce the couple officially married.
                </li>
</ul>
<p>
                While most weddings will involve additional elements, these segments constitute the bare minimum for a legal ceremony. Make sure you cover them!
            </p>
<div class="grid-x grid-padding-x">
<div class="cell small-6 medium-4 large-4 text-center pointer link">
<img alt="Wedding Training" class="thumbnail" src="/assets/ulc/images/resources/wedding-training-540df3ad03.jpg"/>
<a href="/training/wedding-training"><h3 class="h6">Wedding Training</h3></a>
</div>
<div class="cell small-6 medium-4 large-4 text-center pointer link">
<img alt="How to Perform Weddings" class="thumbnail" src="/assets/ulc/images/resources/perform-a-wedding-944f71aaae.jpg"/>
<a href="https://www.ulc.org/training/wedding/how-to-perform-weddings"><h3 class="h6">Perform Weddings</h3></a>
</div>
<div class="cell small-6 small-offset-3 medium-4 medium-offset-0 large-4 text-center pointer link">
<img alt="Wedding Script Generator" class="thumbnail" src="/assets/ulc/images/resources/script-generator-3ec5db7e90.jpg"/>
<a href="/wedding-script"><h3 class="h6">Script Generator</h3></a>
</div>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<h3>Filling Out the Marriage License</h3>
<p>
                After performing the ceremony, make sure to sign the marriage license along with the couple. Marriage
                licenses can be complicated, so be sure to check out our
                <a href="/training/wedding/marriage-license-training">guide to filling out a marriage license</a> to complete this accurately.
                <strong>Important note:</strong> Most states have strict deadlines for returning the license, so be
                aware of this and make sure local officials receive the license within the allotted timeframe.
            </p>
<p>
                Once the paperwork has been submitted, you have officially completed the wedding ceremony.
                Congratulations!
            </p>
<div class="grid-x grid-padding-x">
<div class="cell small-6 medium-4 large-4 text-center pointer link">
<img alt="Marriage Licence Guide" class="thumbnail" src="/assets/ulc/images/resources/marriage-license-guide-81f8b509a6.jpg"/>
<a href="/training/wedding/marriage-license-training"><h3 class="h6">Marriage License Guide</h3></a>
</div>
<div class="cell small-6 medium-4 large-4 text-center pointer link">
<img alt="Wedding Laws" class="thumbnail" src="/assets/ulc/images/resources/wedding-laws-f208aedd26.png"/>
<a href="https://www.ulc.org/wedding-laws"><h3 class="h6">Wedding Laws</h3></a>
</div>
<div class="cell small-6 small-offset-3 medium-4 medium-offset-0 large-4 text-center pointer link">
<img alt="Emergency Wedding Set" class="thumbnail" src="/assets/ulc/images/resources/emergency-wedding-set-09e307f4cb.jpg"/>
<a href="/minister-store/emergency-wedding-set"><h3 class="h6">Emergency Wedding Set</h3></a>
</div>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<h3>After Ceremony Duties</h3>
<p>
                It's common courtesy to stick around for a few minutes after the ceremony to socialize with the couple
                and their guests. This would also be a good time to present the couple with a
                <a href="/minister-store/premium-marriage-certificate">decorative marriage certificate</a>
                commemorating their big day.
            </p>
<div class="grid-x grid-padding-x">
<div class="cell small-6 medium-4 large-4 text-center pointer link">

</div>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<h3>Other Resources</h3>
<p>
                And it doesn't end there! While performing a wedding may be the first thing you do as a ULC Minister,
                your ordination empowers you to do so much more! We would encourage you to explore the rest of this
                site for more training and information, and these three popular pages in particular.
            </p>

<script>
        $(function() {
            $("#ulc-search").submit(function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                window.location = '/search#stq='+$('#ulc-search-submit').val()+'&stp=1';
            });
        });

        function createNotification($form, res, $type="alert") {
            $form.find('.alert').remove();
            let $notification = '<div class="notification ' + $type + ' flex-container align-center align-middle">';
            $notification += '<p class="flex-child">' + res.message + '</p></div>';
            $('body').append($notification);
            if ($type === "success") {
                $('#join-newsletter').slideUp(function() {
                    $form.find('input[type=email]').val('');
                });
            }
        }

        $('#join-newsletter').submit( function(e) {
            const $form = $(e.currentTarget);
            e.preventDefault();
            $.post('/join-newsletter', $form.serialize(), function (res) {
                const type = res.status ? 'success' : 'alert';
                createNotification($form, res, type);
            }).fail(function (error) {
                let response = error.responseJSON;
                let firstEmailError = $.arrayGet(response, 'errors.email.0');
                let message = firstEmailError ? firstEmailError : $.arrayGet(response, 'message');
                createNotification($form, message);
            });
        });
    </script>
<script>
        const ordinationConfirmation = {
            inConfirm: false,

            init: function() {
                $$('#sidebar-ordination').submit(this.handleSubmitAttempt.bind(this));
                $$('body').on('click', '.ordination-confirm .close', this.handleEditClick.bind(this));
                $$('body').on('click', '.ordination-confirm .submit', this.handleConfirmClick.bind(this));
            },
            handleSubmitAttempt: function(e) {
                let name = $('#name').val(),
                    email = $('#email').val(),
                    country = $('#country option:selected').text(),
                    region = $('#region option:selected').text(),
                    filledOut = name && email && $('#country').val() &&
                        ($$('#region').is(':hidden') || $$('#region').val());

                if(filledOut && ! this.inConfirm) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    this.showConfirmModal(name, email, country, region);
                }
            },
            showConfirmModal: function(name, email, country, region) {
                swal({
                    content: $($('#ordination-confirm').html())[0],
                    className: "ordination-confirm",
                    button: false,
                    closeOnClickOutside: false,
                    closeOnEsc: false,
                });

                $('#confirm-name').text(name);
                $('#confirm-email').text(email);
                $('#confirm-country').text(country);
                $('#confirm-region').text(region);

                this.setRegionVisibility();
                $$('html').addClass('disable-scrolling-for-small');
            },
            setRegionVisibility: function() {
                if ($$('#region').is(':hidden')) {
                    $('#confirm-region').hide().prev().hide();
                } else {
                    $('#confirm-region').show().prev().show();
                }
            },
            handleConfirmClick: function() {
                this.inConfirm = true;
                $$('#sidebar-ordination').data('submitted', false);

                $$('html').removeClass('disable-scrolling-for-small');
                $$('#sidebar-ordination').submit();
            },
            handleEditClick: function() {
                this.inConfirm = false;
                $$('#sidebar-ordination').data('submitted', false);
                $$('html').removeClass('disable-scrolling-for-small');

                swal.close();
                beginOrdination();
            },
        };

        const formPointer = {
            init: function() {
                this.setUpShowConditions();
                this.setUpHideConditions();
            },
            setUpShowConditions: function() {
                $$('.goto-form').click(function(e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    beginOrdination();
                });

                if(window.location.hash === '#begin-ordination' ) {
                    beginOrdination();
                }
            },
            setUpHideConditions: function() {
                $$('form').on('click', function() {
                    $$('#ordination-arrow').fadeOut();
                });
            }
        };

        function beginOrdination() {
            let topPos = $$('#sidebar-ordination').length ? $$('#sidebar-ordination').offset().top : 0;
            topPos = topPos > 350 ? topPos : 0;

            $$("html, body").animate({ scrollTop: topPos }, 1000, function() {
                if(! topPos) {
                    $$('#ordination-arrow').fadeIn();
                }
            });
        }

        $(function() {
            ordinationConfirmation.init();
            formPointer.init();
        });
    </script>
<script id="ordination-confirm" type="text/template">
            <div class="wrapper panel">
                <div class="body grid-x grid-margin-x grid-margin-y">
                    <div class="cell small-12">
                        <form method="POST" action="https://www.ulc.org" accept-charset="UTF-8" id="confirmation-form" class="panel"><input name="_token" type="hidden" value="3WrxRLReZklllxMmesOclVEMBxmiSHMugzQsU87S">

<script>
        const setUpOrdinationForm = {
            countryRegions: null,
            protectedCountries: {"AT":"Austria","BE":"Belgium","BG":"Bulgaria","HR":"Croatia","CY":"Cyprus","CZ":"Czechia","DK":"Denmark","EE":"Estonia","FI":"Finland","FR":"France","DE":"Germany","GR":"Greece","HU":"Hungary","IE":"Ireland","IT":"Italy","LV":"Latvia","LT":"Lithuania","LU":"Luxembourg","MT":"Malta","NL":"Netherlands","PL":"Poland","PT":"Portugal","RO":"Romania","SK":"Slovakia","SI":"Slovenia","ES":"Spain","SE":"Sweden","GB":"United Kingdom","CA":"Canada"},

            init: function() {
                $('#sidebar-ordination').preventDoubleSubmission();
                this.getLocationData();
            },
            getLocationData: function() {
                $.ajax({
                    type: 'POST',
                    url: '/get-location-data',
                    data: {
                        _token: Laravel.csrfToken,
                    },
                }).done(function(data) {
                    this.countryRegions = JSON.parse(data.countries_regions);

                    $('body').on('change', '#country', this.handleCountryChange.bind(this));
                    this.updateRegions();
                }.bind(this));
            },
            handleCountryChange: function() {
                this.updateRegions();
                this.setSubscribeCheckedState()
            },
            updateRegions: function() {
                const oldRegion = $$('#region').val() ? $$('#region').val() :
                    ("" ? "" : "");
                updateRegions($$('#country').val(), this.countryRegions, '#region-container', oldRegion);
            },
            setSubscribeCheckedState: function() {
                if (!this.protectedCountries.hasOwnProperty($$('#country').find('option:selected').val())) {
                    $('#subscribe').prop('checked', true);
                } else {
                    $('#subscribe').prop('checked', false);
                }
            },
        };

        $(function (){
            setUpOrdinationForm.init();
        });
    </script>
